As of the date of this report, the Board comprises seven Directors,               於本報告刊發日期，董事會由七名董事組成，
including two executive Directors, two non-executive Directors and                包括兩名執行董事，兩名非執行董事及三名獨
three independent non-executive Directors. Detailed biographical details          立非執行董事。每位董事專業經驗及對本集團
outlining each Director’s range of specialist experience and suitability of       成功長期運作的適合性的簡歷載於本年報標題
the successful long-term operation of the Group are set out in the section        為「董事及高級管理人員」一章節。於二零二四
headed “Directors and Senior Management” of this annual report. Ms.               年六月六日，王瑛女士獲委任為獨立非執行董
Wang Ying was appointed as an independent non-executive Director                  事。王瑛女士於二零二四年六月六日已根據上

The Company was incorporated with limited liability in Hong Kong on 14          本公司為於二零零七年六月十四日在香港註冊

the listing of the shares of the Company on the main board of The Stock         港聯合交易所有限公司（

company of the Group. The shares of the Company commenced listing on            十日開始在聯交所主板上市。

PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES                                                            主要業務
The Company is an investment holding company. The Group is a global             本公司為投資控股公司。本集團是全球領先的
leading provider of high-tech and innovative products, providing customers      高科技創新產品提供商，依託電子信息技術、
around the world with one-stop product solutions relying on its core            人工智能技術、5G和物聯網技術、熱管理技

financial statements that are free from material misstatement, whether due   監控負責。
to fraud or error.

In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors of the     於編製合併財務報表時， 貴公司董事須負責
Company are responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a   評估 貴集團持續經營的能力，並披露（如適
going concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going concern   用）與持續經營有關的事項，以及使用以持續
and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors of      經營為基礎的會計法，除非 貴公司董事有意
the Company either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations or   將 貴集團清盤或停止營運，或並無其他實際
have no realistic alternative but to do so.                                  可行的替代方案。

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                             2.1 編製基準
     These financial statements have been prepared in accordance with                  此等財務報表乃按照由香港會計師公會
     Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRS accounting                       （
                                                                                      「香港會計師公會」     ）頒佈的《香港財務報告
     standards”) (which include all Hong Kong Financial Reporting                      準則》（「《香港財務報告準則會計準則》」         ）
     Standards, Hong Kong Accounting Standards (“HKASs”) and                          （包 括 所 有《香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則》、《香 港
     Interpretations) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public            會計準則》  （
                                                                                              「《香港會計準則》」     ）及詮釋）及
     Accountants (“HKICPA”) and the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                     香港公司條例而編製。此等財務報表乃按
     They have been prepared under the historical cost convention, except              照歷史成本慣例編製，惟若干指定為以公
     for certain equity investments designated at fair value through other             允價值計量並計入其他綜合收益的股權投
     comprehensive income, financial assets at fair value through profit               資、以公允價值計量並計入損益的金融資
     or loss, receivables financing and derivative financial instruments,              產、應收款項融資及衍生金融工具除外，
     which have been measured at fair value. These financial statements                其乃以公允價值計量。此等財務報表以人
     are presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to the               民幣（
                                                                                         「人民幣」  ）呈列，除另有指明外，所有
     nearest thousand except when otherwise indicated.                                 價值乃約整至最接近的千位。


     Basis of consolidation                                                            合併基準
     The consolidated financial statements include the financial statements            合併財務報表包括本集團截至二零二四年
     of the Group for the year ended 31 December 2024. A subsidiary is                 十二月三十一日止年度之財務報表。附屬
     an entity (including a structured entity), directly or indirectly, controlled     公司乃一間由本公司直接或間接控制的實
     by the Company. Control is achieved when the Group is exposed, or                 體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團對參與投
     has rights, to variable returns from its involvement with the investee            資對象業務的浮動回報承擔風險或享有權
     and has the ability to affect those returns through its power over the            利以及能透過對投資對象的權力（即本集團
     investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the current ability to        獲賦予現有能力以主導投資對象相關活動
     direct the relevant activities of the investee).                                  的既存權利）影響該等回報時，即取得控制
                                                                                       權。

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (Continued)                                             2.1 編製基準（續）
  Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                               合併基準（續）
  Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting rights results       一般情況下，假設多數投票權形成控制
  in control. When the Company has less than a majority of the voting or           權。倘本公司擁有少於投資對象大多數投
  similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all relevant facts and        票權或類似權利，則本集團於評估其是否
  circumstances in assessing whether it has power over an investee,                擁有對投資對象的權力時會考慮一切相關
  including:                                                                       事實及情況，包括：

  (a) the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of the               (a) 與投資對象其他投票持有人的合同安
      investee;                                                                        排；

  (b) rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and                      (b) 其他合同安排所產生的權利；及

  (c) the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                       (c) 本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

  The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for the                附屬公司與本公司的財務報表的報告期間
  same reporting period as the Company, using consistent accounting                相同，並採用一致會計政策編製。附屬公
  policies. The results of subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on          司的業績由本集團取得控制權之日起計合
  which the Group obtains control, and continue to be consolidated until           併入賬，並繼續合併入賬至該等控制權終

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                2.4 重大會計政策
  Business combinations and goodwill                                              業務合併及商譽
  Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition                   業務合併乃以收購法入賬。轉讓的代價乃
  method. The consideration transferred is measured at the acquisition            以收購日期的公允價值計算，該公允價值
  date fair value which is the sum of the acquisition date fair values of         為本集團轉讓的資產、本集團自被收購方
  assets transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group to            的前擁有人承擔的負債及本集團發行以換
  the former owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued by            取被收購方控制權的股本權益於收購日期
  the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree. For each business            的公允價值總和。於各業務合併中，本集
  combination, the Group elects whether to measure the non-controlling            團選擇是否以公允價值或被收購方可識別
  interests in the acquiree at fair value or at the proportionate share of        資產淨值的應佔比例，計量於被收購方的
  the acquiree’s identifiable net assets. All other components of non-            非控股權益。非控股權益的所有其他組成
  controlling interests are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related           部分乃以公允價值計量。收購相關成本於
  costs are expensed as incurred.                                                 產生時列為開支。

  The Group determines that it has acquired a business when                       當所購入的一系列業務及資產包括一項投
  the acquired set of activities and assets includes an input and a               入及一項實質性流程，共同對創造產出的
  substantive process that together significantly contribute to the ability       能力作出重大貢獻時，本集團確定其已收
  to create outputs.                                                              購一項業務。

  When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial assets            當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合同條
  and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and designation          款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相關條件，
  in accordance with the contractual terms, economic circumstances                評估將承擔的金融資產及負債，以作出適
  and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the          合的分類及指定，其中包括將被收購方主
  separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the acquiree.           合同中的嵌入式衍生工具進行分離。

  If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously held          倘業務合併分階段進行，先前持有的股權
  equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition date fair value and any        按收購日期的公允價值重新計量，而任何
  resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.                         所得收益或虧損則於損益中確認。

  Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the acquirer                  收購方所轉讓的任何或然代價按於收購日
  is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date. Contingent                 期的公允價值確認。分類為資產或負債的
  consideration classified as an asset or liability is measured at fair value     或然代價以公允價值計量，公允價值變動
  with changes in fair value recognised in profit or loss. Contingent             於損益內確認。分類為權益的或然代價並
  consideration that is classified as equity is not remeasured and                無重新計量，而其後結算於權益中入賬。
  subsequent settlement is accounted for within equity.

      accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for impairment             值虧損後的金額進行計量。商譽每年進行

      Group’s cash-generating units, or groups of cash-generating units,           起，應分配至本集團的每一個現金產生單
      that are expected to benefit from the synergies of the combination,          位或現金產生單位組別，而該等單位或組
      irrespective of whether other assets or liabilities of the Group are         別預期將受益於自合併產生的協同效應，
      assigned to those units or groups of units.                                  無論本集團的其他資產或負債是否分配至
                                                                                   該等單位或單位組別。

      Impairment is determined by assessing the recoverable amount of               減值乃透過評估有關商譽的現金產生單位

      an impairment loss is recognised. An impairment loss recognised for           虧損。已確認的商譽減值虧損將不會於後
      goodwill is not reversed in a subsequent period.                              續期間撥回。

      group of cash-generating units) and part of the operation within             產生單位組別），則當出售該單位部分營運

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                     2.4 重大會計政策（續）
  Fair value measurement                                                          公允價值計量
  The Group measures its equity investments and derivative financial              本集團於各報告期末以公允價值計量其股
  instruments at fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair value       本投資及衍生金融工具。公允價值指市場
  is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer        參與者於計量日期在有序交易中出售資產
  a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants at            所收取或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公允價
  the measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on the                值計量所依據的假設為，出售資產或轉讓
  presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability    負債的交易乃於該資產或負債的主要市場
  takes place either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or in    進行，或如無主要市場，則於對該資產或
  the absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous market              負債最為有利的市場進行。本集團必須可
  for the asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market       進入該主要或最為有利的市場。計量資產
  must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability      或負債的公允價值使用市場參與者於為該
  is measured using the assumptions that market participants would use            資產或負債定價時所依據的假設，即假設
  when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market participants          市場參與者按其最佳經濟利益行事。
  act in their economic best interest.

  A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account            非金融資產的公允價值計量參考市場參與
  a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using           者可從使用該資產得到的最高及最佳效
  the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market        用，或把該資產售予另一可從使用該資產
  participant that would use the asset in its highest and best use.               得到最高及最佳效用的市場參與者所產生
                                                                                  的經濟效益。

  The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the                 本集團使用適用於不同情況的估值方法，
  circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to measure            而其有足夠數據可計量公允價值，並盡量
  fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and                利用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少使用
  minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                                      不可觀察輸入數據。

      In calculating the present value of lease payments, the Group                  計算租賃付款的現值時，由於租賃中
      uses its incremental borrowing rate at the lease commencement                  所隱含的利率不易釐定，故本集團於
      date because the interest rate implicit in the lease is not readily            租賃開始日期使用其遞增借貸利率。
      determinable. After the commencement date, the amount of                       於開始日期後，租賃負債金額增加，
      lease liabilities is increased to reflect the accretion of interest and        以反映利息的增加及所作出的租賃付
      reduced for the lease payments made. In addition, the carrying                 款減少。此外，倘存在修改、租期變
      amount of lease liabilities is remeasured if there is a modification,          動、租賃付款變動（例如：由指數或比
      a change in the lease term, a change in lease payments (e.g., a                率變動引起的未來租賃付款變動）或購
      change to future lease payments resulting from a change in an                  買相關資產的選擇權的評估變動，則
      index or rate) or a change in assessment of an option to purchase              重新計量租賃負債的賬面價值。
      the underlying asset.

  In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured at                為使金融資產以攤餘成本或以公允價值計
  amortised cost or fair value through other comprehensive income, it            量並計入其他綜合收益進行分類及計量，

  at fair value through profit or loss, irrespective of the business model.      類及計量，不論其業務模式如何。

  The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers to            本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其管理
  how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash flows.          其金融資產以產生現金流量的方式。業務
  The business model determines whether cash flows will result from             模式釐定現金流量是否來自收集合同現金
  collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial assets, or both.     流量、出售金融資產，或兩者兼有。以攤
  Financial assets classified and measured at amortised cost are held           餘成本分類及計量的金融資產於旨在持有
  within a business model with the objective to hold financial assets in        金融資產於以收取合同現金流量的業務模
  order to collect contractual cash flows, while financial assets classified    式中持有，而以公允價值分類及計量並計
  and measured at fair value through other comprehensive income are             入其他綜合收益的金融資產於旨在持有以
  held within a business model with the objective of both holding to            收取合同現金流量及銷售的業務模式中持
  collect contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which are        有。並非於上述業務模式持有的金融資產
  not held within the aforementioned business models are classified and         以公允價值分類及計量並計入損益。
  measured at fair value through profit or loss.

  Purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery of assets        按照一般市場規定或慣例須在一定期間內

  Impairment of financial assets                                                   金融資產減值
  The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses                      本集團確認對並非持作以公允價值計量並
  (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through profit           計入損益的所有債務工具的預期信貸虧損
  or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the contractual                （
                                                                                   「預期信貸虧損」 ）的一項撥備。預期信貸
  cash flows due in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows             虧損乃基於根據合同到期的合同現金流量
  that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an approximation                 與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量之間的
  of the original effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will             差額而釐定，並以原實際利率的近似值折
  include cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit               現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押的
  enhancements that are integral to the contractual terms.                          現金流量或組成合同條款的其他信貸提升
                                                                                    措施的現金流量。

  of recovering the contractual cash flows.                                   銷金融資產。

  subject to impairment under the general approach and they are               產根據一般方式須予減值，並按以下為計
  classified within the following stages for measurement of ECLs except       量預期信貸虧損的階段分類，惟應用簡化

  approach as detailed below.                                                 產除外。

  Stage 1   –   Financial instruments for which credit risk has not           階段1 － 金融工具的信貸風險自初步確認
                increased significantly since initial recognition and for           後並未顯著增加，且虧損撥備按
                which the loss allowance is measured at an amount                   相等於12個月預期信貸虧損金額
                equal to 12-month ECLs                                              計量


  Stage 2   –   Financial instruments for which credit risk has increased     階段2 － 金融工具的信貸風險自初步確認
                significantly since initial recognition but that are not            後顯著增加，惟其並非已發生信
                credit-impaired financial assets and for which the loss             貸減值的金融資產，且虧損撥備
                allowance is measured at an amount equal to lifetime                按相等於年限內預期信貸虧損金
                ECLs                                                                額計量


  Stage 3   –   Financial assets that are credit-impaired at the reporting    階段3 － 金融資產於報告日期為信貸減值
                date (but that are not purchased or originated credit-             （惟其並非購買或原已發生信貸
                impaired) and for which the loss allowance is measured              減值的金融工具），且虧損撥備
                at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs                                 按相等於年限內預期信貸虧損金
                                                                                    額計量

  Simplified approach                                                         簡化法
  For trade receivables that do not contain a significant financing           就不包含顯著融資組成部分或倘本集團應
  component or when the Group applies the practical expedient of              用不調整顯著融資組成部分影響的實際權
  not adjusting the effect of a significant financing component, the          宜方法的應收貿易款項，本集團應用簡化
  Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the        法計算預期信貸虧損。根據簡化法，本集
  simplified approach, the Group does not track changes in credit risk,       團並無追蹤信貸風險的變動，反而於各報
  but instead recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECLs at           告日期根據年限內預期信貸虧損確認虧
  each reporting date. The Group has established a provision matrix that      損撥備。本集團已根據其以往信貸虧損經
  is based on its historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-    驗，建立撥備矩陣，並就債務人及經濟環
  looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic environment.       境的特定前瞻性因素作出調整。

  Provisions                                                                   撥備
  A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or                倘因過去事件而須承擔現有責任（法定或推
  constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is probable      定），且很可能須以日後資源流出清償此等
  that a future outflow of resources will be required to settle the            責任，且惟責任金額可作出可靠估計時，
  obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the             則會確認撥備。
  amount of the obligation.

  The Group provides for warranties in relation to the sale of certain         本集團就銷售特定工業產品及擔保期間產
  industrial products for general repairs of defects occurring during          生瑕疵的一般性維修提供擔保。本集團所
  the warranty period. Provisions for these assurance-type warranties          提供的保證型擔保計提的撥備乃按銷量及
  granted by the Group are initially recognised based on sales volume          過往的維修及退回情況初步確認入賬，並
  and past experience of the level of repairs and returns, discounted          折現至其現值（倘適用）。擔保相關成本每
  to their present values as appropriate. The warranty related cost is         年予以修訂。
  revised annually.

  Group operates.

  differences at the end of the reporting period between the tax bases of      務報告賬面價值之間的所有暫時性差異，

  Government grants                                                              政府補貼
  Government grants are recognised at their fair value where there is            政府補貼是在合理確保將可收取補貼及符

  item, it is recognised as income on a systematic basis over the periods        補貼於擬用作補償的成本支出期間內按系
  that the costs, for which it is intended to compensate, are expensed.          統基礎確認為收入。

  Where the grant relates to an asset, the fair value is credited to a           倘補貼與一項資產有關，則其公允價值會
  deferred income account and is released to the statement of profit             計入遞延損益賬，並按均等年度分期，於
  or loss over the expected useful life of the relevant asset by equal           有關資產的預期可使用年期調撥至損益
  annual instalments or deducted from the carrying amount of the asset           表，或自該項資產的賬面價值中扣除並透
  and released to the statement of profit or loss by way of a reduced            過減少折舊開支方式調撥至損益表。
  depreciation charge.


  Revenue recognition                                                            收入確認
  Revenue from contracts with customers                                          客戶合同收入
  Revenue from contracts with customers is recognised when control               當貨品或服務控制權按反映本集團預期有
  of goods or services is transferred to the customers at an amount that         權獲得以交換該等貨品或服務的代價金額
  reflects the consideration to which the Group expects to be entitled in        轉移至客戶時，確認客戶合同收入。
  exchange for those goods or services.

  The Group sells products to a customer who is also the supplier                本集團向特定客戶銷售產品，該客戶同時
  of key materials used in the manufacturing of products. The Group              也是產品製造過程中使用的關鍵材料的供
  obtains the control of the materials purchased from the customer and           應商。本集團取得自客戶採購的材料的控
  provides significant services to integrate materials with other goods          制權並提供重要服務，從而將材料與其他
  and services into a portfolio of outputs. The Group considered itself as       商品及服務綜合為一個產出組合。本集團
  a principal in the arrangement and accordingly recognises revenue on           在該安排中視其為主要責任人，因此按總
  a gross basis.                                                                 額基準確認收入。

      The Group uses a provision matrix to calculate ECLs for trade                  本集團使用撥備矩陣來計算應收貿易款項
      receivables. The provision rates are based on aging for groupings of           的預期信貸虧損。撥備率乃基於具有類似
      various customer segments that have similar loss patterns.                     虧損模式的各個客戶群的分組的賬齡。

      The provision matrix is initially based on the Group’s historical              撥備矩陣初步基於本集團的過往觀察違約
      observed default rates. The Group will calibrate the matrix to adjust          率作出。本集團將使用前瞻性信息校準該
      the historical credit loss experience with forward-looking information.        矩陣以調整過往信貸虧損經歷。例如，倘
      For instance, if forecast economic conditions (i.e., gross domestic            預測經濟狀況（即國內生產總值）預計將於
      products) are expected to deteriorate over the next year which can             下一年惡化（其可能導致製造行業違約數量
      lead to an increased number of defaults in the manufacturing sector,           增加），則將調整過往違約率。於各報告日
      the historical default rates are adjusted. At each reporting date, the         期，本集團會更新過往觀察違約率並分析
      historical observed default rates are updated and changes in the               前瞻性估計值的變化。
      forward-looking estimates are analysed.

      The assessment of the correlation among historical observed default            對過往觀察違約率、預測經濟狀況及預期
      rates, forecast economic conditions and ECLs is a significant estimate.        信貸虧損之間相關性的評估乃為一項重要
      The amount of ECLs is sensitive to changes in circumstances and                估計。預期信貸虧損的數量對環境變化及
      forecast economic conditions. The Group’s historical credit loss               預測經濟條件較為敏感。本集團的過往信
      experience and forecast of economic conditions may also not be                 貸虧損經驗及經濟狀況預測亦可能無法代

      information about the ECLs on the Group’s trade receivables is                 應收貿易款項的預期信貸虧損的資料於財
      disclosed in note 20 to the financial statements.                              務報表附註20中披露。

      for impairment annually and at other times when such an indicator              該跡象時進行減值測試。其他非金融資產
      exists. Other non-financial assets are tested for impairment when              則於出現賬面價值可能不可收回的跡象時
      there are indicators that the carrying amounts may not be recoverable.         進行減值測試。資產或現金產生單位的賬
      An impairment exists when the carrying value of an asset or a cash-            面價值超逾可收回金額（即其公允價值減出
      generating unit exceeds its recoverable amount, which is the higher of         售成本及使用價值兩者中之較高者）時，則
      its fair value less costs of disposal and its value in use. The calculation    出現減值。計量公允價值減出售成本時，
      of the fair value less costs of disposal is based on available data            按約束銷售交易根據公平合理基準交易類
      from binding sales transactions in an arm’s length transaction of              似資產可得數據，或可得市價減出售資產
      similar assets or observable market prices less incremental costs for          所產生的應計費用而得出。當計算使用價

      Leases – Estimating the incremental borrowing rate                             租賃－估算增量借款利率
      The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a lease,      本集團無法輕易釐定租賃內所隱含的利
      and therefore, it uses an incremental borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure        率，因此，使用增量借款利率計量租賃負
      lease liabilities. The IBR is the rate of interest that the Group would        債。增量借款利率為本集團於類似經濟環
      have to pay to borrow over a similar term, and with a similar security,        境中為取得與使用權資產價值相近之資
      the funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar value to the right-of-     產，而以類似抵押品於類似期間借入所需
      use asset in a similar economic environment. The IBR therefore reflects        資金應支付之利率。因此，增量借款利率
      what the Group “would have to pay”, which requires estimation when             反映了本集團「應支付」的利率，當無可觀
      no observable rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that do not        察的利率時（如就並無訂立融資交易之附屬
      enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to be adjusted to          公司而言）或當須對利率進行調整以反映租
      reflect the terms and conditions of the lease (for example, when leases        賃之條款及條件時（如當租賃並非以附屬公
      are not in the subsidiary’s functional currency). The Group estimates          司的功能貨幣計價時），則須作出估計。當
      the IBR using observable inputs (such as market interest rates) when           可觀察輸入數據可用時，本集團使用可觀
      available and is required to make certain entity-specific estimates (such      察輸入數據（如市場利率）估算增量借款利
      as the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).                                率，並須針對不同實體作出若干估計（例如
                                                                                     附屬公司的單獨信貸評級）。

      Fair value of identifiable net assets and goodwill acquired through            透過業務合併收購的可識別資產淨值的公

      difference between the transaction costs of the merger and the fair            交易成本與公允價值之間的差額確認為商
      value is recognised as goodwill.                                               譽。

      Assessing the fair value of identifiable assets and liabilities involves       評估可識別資產及負債的公允價值涉及管
      significant management estimates and judgements, in particular the             理層作出重大估計及判斷，尤其是識別無
      identification of intangible assets and the assessment of their fair value,    形資產及評估其公允價值，會影響商譽的
      which affects the recognition of goodwill.                                     確認。

      The assessment of the fair value of identifiable net assets as at the          評估可識別資產淨值於收購日期的公允價
      date of acquisition, including the identification of intangible assets, the    值（包括識別無形資產、選擇估值方法及預
      selection of valuation methods, and the prediction of future cash flows,       測未來現金流量等）涉及對收入增長率、毛
      etc., involves significant estimates and judgements of key assumptions         利率及折現率等關鍵假設作出重大估計及
      such as revenue growth rate, gross profit margin and discount rate.            判斷。關鍵假設中所使用輸入數據的差異
      Differences in the inputs used in key assumptions could lead to                可能導致公允價值估算出現巨大差異。
      substantial differences in fair value estimates.

      The Group’s corporate finance team headed by the finance manager              財務經理領導的本集團公司財務團隊負責
      is responsible for determining the policies and procedures for the fair       制定金融工具公允價值計量的政策及程
      value measurement of financial instruments. The corporate finance             序。公司財務團隊直接向財務總監報告。
      team reports directly to the chief financial officer. At each reporting       於各報告日期，公司財務團隊分析金融工
      date, the corporate finance team analyses the movements in the                具價值的變動，並確定在估值中應用的主
      values of financial instruments and determines the major inputs applied       要輸入值。估值由財務總監審核及批准。
      in the valuation. The valuation is reviewed and approved by the chief
      financial officer.

      The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are included at       金融資產及負債的公允價值以該工具於自
      the amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in a current            願交易方（而非強迫或清盤銷售）當前交易
      transaction between willing parties, other than in a forced or liquidation    下的可交易金額入賬。下述方法及假設用
      sale. The following methods and assumptions were used to estimate             於評估公允價值：
      the fair values:

      The fair values of the unlisted equity investments have been valued           非上市股權投資的公允價值按市場法進行
      based on a market–based valuation technique. This valuation requires          估值。該估值要求本集團釐定可資比較上
      the Group to determine the comparable listed companies, select the            市公司、選擇價格倍數並對缺乏流動性折
      price multiple, and make estimates about the discount for lack of             價進行估計，因此具有不確定性。
      liquidity, and hence they are subject to uncertainty.


      The fair values of the interest–bearing loans have been calculated            計息貸款的公允價值是通過使用具有類似
      by discounting the expected future cash flows using rates currently           條款、信貸風險和剩餘到期日的工具的當
      available for instruments with similar terms, credit risk and remaining       前可得利率折現預期未來現金流量來計
      maturities. The changes in fair value as a result of the Group’s own          算。於二零二四年十二月三十一日，由於
      non–performance risk for interest–bearing loans as at 31 December             本集團對計息貸款的非履約風險而導致的
      2024 were assessed to be insignificant.                                       公允價值變動評估為並不重大。

      The Group enters into derivative financial instruments and receivables        本集團與多個對手方訂立衍生金融工具及
      financing with various counterparties. The carrying amounts of these          應收款項融資。該等衍生金融工具及應收
      derivative financial instruments and receivables financing are the same       款項融資的賬面價值與彼等的公允價值相
      as their fair values. The derivative financial instruments and receivables    同。衍生金融工具及應收款項融資以現值
      financing are measured using valuation techniques similar to forward          計算並按遠期價格相似之估值技巧計量。
      pricing, using present value calculations. The models incorporate             此等模式計入不同市場可觀察輸入數據，
      various market observable inputs including the credit quality of              包括對手方信貸質素，以及外匯即期及遠
      counterparties and foreign exchange spot and forward rates. The               期匯率。該等衍生金融工具及應收款項融
      carrying amounts of derivative financial instruments and receivables          資的賬面價值與其公允價值相同。
      financing are the same as their fair values.

38. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES AND                                    38. 財務風險管理目標及政策
    POLICIES
      The Group’s principal financial instruments, other than derivatives,       本集團的主要金融工具（衍生工具除外）包
      comprise loans, balances with related companies and cash and bank          括貸款、與關連公司的結餘以及現金及銀
      balances. The main purpose of these financial instruments is to raise      行結餘。此等金融工具的主要用途乃為本
      finance for the Group’s operations. The Group has various other            集團的營運籌集資金。本集團有其他不同
      financial assets and liabilities such as trade receivables and trade       金融資產及負債，如直接自其營運產生的
      payables, which arise directly from its operations.                        應收貿易款項及應付貿易賬款。


      The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are          本集團金融工具所產生的主要風險為外匯
      foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. The board of        風險、信貸風險及流動資金風險。董事會
      directors reviews and agrees policies for managing each of these risks     審閱並同意下文所概述的管理各項此等風
      and they are summarised below.                                             險的政策。


      Interest rate risk                                                         利率風險
      At 31 December 2024 and 2023, the Group has no significant interest        於二零二四年及二零二三年十二月三十一
      risk as the Group did not have long–term debt obligations with floating    日，本集團並無重大利率風險，乃因為本
      interest rates.                                                            集團並無浮息長期債務責任。


      Foreign currency risk                                                      外匯風險
      The Group has transactional currency exposures. Such exposures             本集團有交易貨幣風險。有關風險來自經
      arise from sales or purchases by operating units in currencies other       營單位以並非該單位功能貨幣的貨幣進行
      than the units’ functional currencies. The Group tends to accept           買賣。本集團傾向於在訂立買賣合同時接
      foreign currency exchange risk avoidance or allocation terms when          納外匯風險規避或分配條款。本集團對外
      arriving at purchase and sale contracts. The Group takes rolling           匯收入及開支實行滾動預測，並匹配貨幣
      forecast on the foreign currency revenue and expenses and matches          與費用金額，以減輕匯率波動對業務產生
      the currency and the amount incurred so as to alleviate the impact on      的影響。
      business due to exchange rate fluctuations.

  Capital management                                                                  資本管理
  The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are to                     本集團資本管理的主要目標為確保本集團
  safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern and to                 持續發展的能力及保持良好的資本比率，
  maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its business and                以支持本集團的業務及盡量提升股東價
  maximise shareholders’ value.                                                       值。

  The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments to                    本集團根據經濟狀況的變化管理其資本架
  it in light of changes in economic conditions. To maintain or adjust                構並作出調整。為保持或調整資本架構，
  the capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend payment                    本集團或會調整派付予股東的股息、股東
  to shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue new shares.                的資本回報或發行新股。截至二零二四年
  No changes were made in the objectives, policies or processes for                   十二月三十一日及二零二三年十二月三十
  managing capital during the years ended 31 December 2024 and 31                     一日止年度，並無對管理資本的目標、政
  December 2023.                                                                      策或程序作出更改。

  The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is net debt                 本集團以資本負債比率（即債務淨值除以權
  divided by equity. The Group’s policy is to maintain the gearing ratio              益）監察其資本。本集團的政策為將資本負
  as low as possible.                                                                 債比率盡可能保持最低。

40. TRANSFERS OF FINANCIAL ASSETS                                                  40. 金融資產轉移
      Financial assets that are derecognised in their entirety                       完全撤銷確認的金融資產
      In the ordinary course of business, the Group has factored a part              本集團在日常業務中，因短期融資需求將
      of receivables to financial institutions without recourse for its short–       部分應收賬款以無追索權方式保理給金融
      term financing needs, and has entered into non–recourse receivables            機構，並與多家銀行訂立無追索權的應收
      factoring agreements with a number of banks to transfer certain                賬款保理協議，將若干應收賬款轉讓予該
      receivables to those banks (the “Receivable Factoring”). Under                 等銀行（
                                                                                        「應收賬款保理」
                                                                                               ）。在若干應收賬款
      certain receivable factoring agreements, the Group is not required             保理協議下，本集團無須承擔應收賬款轉
      to undertake default risks and the delayed repayment risk from the             讓後的債務人違約風險及延遲還款風險，
      debtors after the transfer of receivables, and all risks and rewards           且已轉移應收賬款所有權之所有的風險和
      related to the ownership of the receivables are transferred. The               報酬，符合終止金融資產條件。因此，本
      definition of termination of financial assets is met. Therefore, the Group     集團對保理協議下的應收賬款按照賬面價
      derecognised the receivables under the factoring agreements at                 值撤銷確認。
      carrying amount.

      There is no such factoring arrangement during the current year.                於本年度並無有關保理安排。
